Memory of
The Late Rioht Reverend R. S. Coplbstone, D.D.,
Late Metropolitan of India & Ceylon
AND
Late Mrs. Coplbstone
Knm ka jingkynmaw ieit bad burom ia u Right
Ri'x. R. S. Coplestone, d.d., bad ia i Mrs. Coplestone, kiba
la iap, la sngewdei ban phah shon biang ia kane ka Kot
“Lain pa ii Lain.”
Mynba u Bishop Coplestone u dang trci ha Calcutta
kum u Metropolitan jong ka India bad Ceylon, i
Mrs. Coplt'stone i la siew tulop ia uwci u Nong-Sohra ba
nn pynwan iakaKot “Line upon Line” sha ka ktien Khasi.
Bad yii<la la dep, i la phah shon ia ka kot ha ka Baptist
Mission Press, Calcutta, ha ka snem 1909 da kaba aiew ia
ka jinglut na lade hi.
Dei u Bishop Coplestone uba ia pynbeit ban phiah
noh ia ka Calcutta Diocese. Bad ba ka Assam kan ioh la
u jong u Bishop, uba kyrpang. Kumta, kumba ar snem
hadien ba u la mih na India, la kha ia ka Assam Diocese
ha ka snem 1916.
Shaphang une u Bishop la ju ong ba u long u **Saint
and Scholar.” U long ruh u Linguist (ba nang ia ki ktien
nongwei). Hadien ba u la don shibnai ha Shillong u la lah
ban iathuhkhana ha ka ktien Khasi, I Mrs. Coplestone
i khun jong u Archbishop Trench, i don ka dohnud ba sbun
bad ba sngewlcm ia kiba duk ba rangli — i la leh isynei
shibun iaki ba duk ha Calcutta bad ha ki jaka baroh kat
sha kiba i la poi. La ju kren shaphang jong i kum “i kmio
jong kiba duk.*'

Nga sngow ba ngam lah ban klet katba dang im ia
kine ki sliakri U Trai, kiba la shongthait inynta, iia ki
jingtroi shitom jong ki, bad kiba la iohnoh ha ngi ia ka
nuksa ka jingim riowblei da ki jinghikai bad ka jinglong
jingim jong ki.
Bad (la kaba ngeit ba kane ka kot “Lain pa u Lain”
kan iarap ia ki kuu(^ ki kpa bad ia ki nonghikai skul ban
liikai ia la ki khun })ad ki khyniiah skill ba kin nang roi ha
ka jiiiglip *a ka Ktien U Bici, iigi la sngowshlur ban pbah
shon biang ia kane ka kot “Lain pa u Lain,” ba kan h^ng
ruh iLuni ka dak ka jingsngewriguh jong ka Asscam Diocese
bad khamtara jong kane ka Ri Lum jong ngi, ia kine ki
shakri U Trai, kiba la phah pynwan ia ka kot Phareng
“Line upon Line” sha ka ktien jong ngi ki Kiiasi.
Dated Jowai, \ L. GATPHOH,
The 19th July 1905. J Arch De voon, Assam Diocese,
